# ✍️ Writer — 개발된 영상 콘텐츠 아이디어를 바탕으로 간결한 스크립트를 작성하며, 사용자의 입장권을 고려해 즐길 수 있도록 내용을 구성

### 영상 콘텐츠 스크립트: "The AI-Human Collab" (AI와 인간의 협업 시리즈)

---

#### **1. Introduction**
- **Title:** The AI-Human Collab
- **Opening Scene:** Brief introduction of the concept.
  - *Narrator:* "Welcome to 'The AI-Human Collab,' where we challenge the boundaries of creativity by pitting human ingenuity against cutting-edge artificial intelligence."
  
#### **2. Prompt Engineering and Mission Setup**
- **Scene Transition:** Cut to a brainstorming room with agents or PDs discussing mission parameters.
  - *PD:* "Today's mission is simple yet daunting: can our AI counterparts create compelling YouTube Shorts within a budget of $10, and how will they fare against human creativity?"
  
#### **3. Prompt Release and Execution**
- **Scene Transition:** Cut to an office setting where the PD inputs prompts into the AI system.
  - *PD:* "Let's see what happens when we give this AI a challenge it can't refuse."
  - *(Narrator Voiceover)*: "Watch as the PD crafts detailed instructions for our AI, specifying that it must create ten engaging YouTube Shorts within a day using only $10 worth of resources."

#### **4. AI Execution and Feedback Loop**
- **Scene Transition:** Switch to real-time footage or simulations showing how AI processes information and creates content.
  - *Narrator:* "The AI gets straight to work, analyzing data trends and creating unique content based on user engagement patterns."
  
- **Cut-in Scene:** Human reviewers critique the AI-generated Shorts.
  - *(Reviewer):* "While the AI certainly has a knack for efficiency, there's something about human creativity that can't be replicated."

#### **5. Final Comparison and Conclusion**
- **Scene Transition:** Show side-by-side comparisons of AI-generated content versus human-made content.
  - *PD:* "Now we compare both sets to see which approach truly resonates with audiences."
  
- **Closing Scene: Reflection and Forward-Looking Statement**
  - *(Narrator):* "Through this experiment, we've discovered that while technology offers incredible possibilities, it's the blend of human intuition and machine precision that creates something truly special."

---

#### **3. Prompt Engineering and Mission Setup**

**Scene Transition:** Cut to a brainstorming room where multiple PDs are discussing potential missions for future episodes.

- *PD1:* "Next week's challenge could be 'Can AI recreate classic short films using limited resources?'"
- *PD2:* "Or, perhaps we ask the AI to come up with entirely new narratives based on user-generated prompts!"

---

#### **4. Future Mission Teasers**

**Scene Transition:** Quick teaser clips showing hypothetical missions for upcoming episodes.

- *(Teaser Clip 1)*: A scene where an AI is given a prompt to recreate a famous movie trailer using only found footage and text-to-speech technology.
- *(Teaser Clip 2)*: An episode where users submit their own challenges, and the AI tries its hand at solving them under strict constraints.

---

#### **5. Closing Call-to-Action**

**Scene Transition:** Final scene with a call to action for viewers to participate in future challenges.

- *(Narrator):* "Join us next week as we continue to explore the endless possibilities of human-AI collaboration."
  
---

### 스크립트 요약 및 실행 방안

1. **프롬프트 엔지니어링:** PD가 AI에게 특정 미션을 수행할 수 있는 프롬프트를 생성하고, 그 내용을 공개.
2. **실행 과정 기록:** AI의 모든 작업과 결과물을 촬영 및 녹화하여 시청자와 공유.
3. **결과물 비교 및 분석:** AI와 인간의 작품을 직접 비교하며 서로의 장점을 강조.

---

### 추가 제안
- **시리즈 연속성 보장:** 각 에피소드에서 발생한 핵심 아이디어와 문제를 다음 에피소드에 적용하여 시청자의 흥미를 유지.
- **참여형 콘텐츠:** 사용자들에게 미션 제안을 받는 섹션을 추가하여 더욱 개인화된 경험 제공.

---

### 스크립트 검토 및 피드백
이 스크립트는 AI와 인간의 협업 과정을 통해 시청자가 참여할 수 있는 '지적 유희'를 제공합니다. 또한, 프롬프트 엔지니어링과 같은 기술적 측면도 강조하여 미래 트렌드에 대비한 유튜브 콘텐츠의 방향성을 제시합니다.

- **AI 시나리오 제안:** AI 에이전트에게 다음 에피소드의 프롬프트와 미션 생성을 의뢰합니다.
